debug_decisions = {
	faith_debug_decision = {
		complete_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { faith_points = 500 }
			add_stability = -0.5
		}
	}
	nuke_debug_decision = {
		complete_effect = {
			add_nuclear_bombs = 5
			complete_special_project = sp:sp_magic_mana_reactor
			complete_special_project = sp:sp_magic_mana_bomb
			set_technology = {
				bomber_breeds_1 = 1
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = organic_bomber_equipment_1
				amount = 50
			}
		}
	}
	compliance_debug_decision = {
		complete_effect = {
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT } }
				add_compliance = 100
			}
			#load_focus_tree = nexus_war_alt
			set_country_flag = nexus_war_flag
		}
	}
}

religion_decisions = {

	#########################
	# Accepting Religion
	#########################
	accept_church_of_the_holy_light_decision = {

		available = {
			NOT = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = accepted_religions_cap_tt
					check_variable = { accepted_religions^num > 2 }
				}
				has_country_flag = accepting_a_religion
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = state_religion_tt
					check_variable = { religion = 1 }
				}
			}
		}

		visible = {
			AND = {
				any_owned_state = {
					check_variable = { religion = 1 }
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_idea = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
						has_idea = secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
					}
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = no

		cost = 300
		days_remove = 365
		days_re_enable = 365

		remove_effect = {
			add_ideas = { secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light }
			set_temp_variable = { religion_to_accept = 1 }
			add_religion_as_accepted_religion = yes
			clr_country_flag = accepting_a_religion
		}

		complete_effect = { set_country_flag = accepting_a_religion }

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1
				has_political_power > 320 #Spend on other things first
			}
		}
	}

	banish_the_church_of_the_holy_light_decision = {

		available = { has_idea = secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light }
		visible = { has_idea = secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light }

		fire_only_once = no

		cost = 300
		days_remove = 90
		days_re_enable = 30

		remove_effect = {
			add_stability = -0.1
			remove_ideas = { secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light }
			set_temp_variable = { religion_to_unaccept = 1 }
			remove_religion_as_accepted_religion = yes
			clr_country_flag = removing_a_religion
		}

		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = removing_a_religion
		}
	}

		days_re_enable = 30

		}

		}

	convert_to_the_church_of_the_holy_light_decision = {

		available = { has_idea = secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light }
		visible = { has_idea = secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light }

		fire_only_once = no

		cost = 300
		days_remove = 365
		days_re_enable = 365

		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = convert_to_the_church_of_the_holy_light_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_ideas = secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
				meta_effect = {
					text = { set_variable = { main_religion = [MAIN_RELIGION] } }
					MAIN_RELIGION = "[ROOT.GetMainReligionVariable]"
				}
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_ideas = [TARGET_RELIGION_AS_SECONDARY] }
					TARGET_RELIGION_AS_SECONDARY = "[ROOT.GetSecondaryReligionIdea]"
				}
				add_ideas = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			add_stability = -0.1
		}
	}

church_of_the_holy_light_decisions = {

	faith_in_the_light = {
		icon = tur_the_constitutional_arrows

		custom_cost_trigger = {
			set_temp_variable = { temp_faith_cost = 100 }
			check_variable = { faith_points > temp_faith_cost }
		}
		custom_cost_text = "religious_decisions_cost_text"

		visible = {
			has_idea = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
			#OR = {
			#	has_idea = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
			#	has_idea = secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
			#}
		}

		available = {
			has_idea = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
			#OR = {
			#	has_idea = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
			#	has_idea = secondary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
			#}
			check_variable = { faith_points > 100 }
			OR = {
				has_war_support < 0.7
				has_stability < 0.8
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = faith_points value = 100 }
		}

		modifier = {
			stability_weekly = 0.003
			war_support_weekly = 0.005
		}

		days_remove = 90
		days_re_enable = 90

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1
				has_stability < 0.6
				has_war_support < 0.6
			}
		}
	}

	divine_warriors = {
		icon = tur_the_constitutional_arrows

		custom_cost_trigger = {
			set_temp_variable = { temp_faith_cost = 100 }
			check_variable = { faith_points > temp_faith_cost }
		}
		custom_cost_text = "religious_decisions_cost_text"

		visible = {
			has_idea = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
		}

		available = {
			has_war = yes
			has_idea = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
			check_variable = { faith_points > 100 }
		}

		complete_effect = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = faith_points value = 100 }
		}

		### Gives about 720 manpower, enough for a 20-width and some change
		modifier = {
			army_core_defence_factor = 0.1
			weekly_manpower = 60
		}

		days_remove = 90
		days_re_enable = 90

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
	}

	the_lights_charge = {
		icon = tur_the_constitutional_arrows

		custom_cost_trigger = {
			set_temp_variable = { temp_faith_cost = 100 }
			check_variable = { faith_points > temp_faith_cost }
		}
		custom_cost_text = "religious_decisions_cost_text"

		visible = {
			has_idea = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
		}

		available = {
			has_war = yes
			has_idea = primary_religion_the_church_of_the_holy_light
			check_variable = { faith_points > 100 }
		}

		complete_effect = {

		}

			breakthrough_factor = 0.15
			army_speed_factor = 0.1
		}

		days_re_enable = 90

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
	}

	light_the_forges = {
		icon = tur_the_constitutional_arrows

		custom_cost_trigger = {
			set_temp_variable = { temp_faith_cost = 100 }
			check_variable = { faith_points > temp_faith_cost }

		custom_cost_text = "religious_decisions_cost_text"

			has_idea = primary_religion_followers_of_the_tidehunter
			check_variable = { faith_points > 100 }
			ROOT = {
				any_owned_state = {
					is_coastal = yes
					impassable = no
					is_core_of = ROOT
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					random_owned_state = {
						limit = {
							is_coastal = yes
							impassable = no
							is_core_of = ROOT
						}
						add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
						add_building_construction = {
							type = dockyard
							level = 2
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
				}
				50 = {
					random_owned_state = {
						limit = {
							is_coastal = yes
							impassable = no
							is_core_of = ROOT
						}
						add_building_construction = {
							type = coastal_bunker
							level = 1
							instant_build = yes
							province = {
								all_provinces = yes
								limit_to_coastal = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = faith_points value = 100 }
		}

		days_remove = 90
		days_re_enable = 90

		ai_will_do = { factor = 1 }
	}

			has_war = yes

							}
						}

							idea = necrotic_plague_idea
							days = 90
						}
					}
				}
				every_allied_country = {
					add_timed_idea = {
						idea = necrotic_plague_idea_garrison
						days = 90
					}
				}
				else = {
					custom_effect_tooltip = release_the_plague_decision_tt
				}
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = faith_points value = 100 }
		}

		days_remove = 90

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
	}

					days = 90
				}
			}

					days = 90
				}
			}

			every_enemy_country = {

					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_idea = primary_religion_the_void
							has_idea = vampiric_touch_debuff_idea
						}

		    set_temp_variable = { pop_to_kill = manpower }
		    divide_temp_variable = { pop_to_kill = 25 }
		    round_temp_variable = pop_to_kill
		    multiply_temp_variable = { pop_to_kill = -1 }
		    add_manpower = pop_to_kill
		}

		modifier = {
			stability_weekly = 0.017 #20
			war_support_weekly = 0.017 #20
		}

		days_remove = 90

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1
				has_stability < 0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 1
				has_war_support < 0.5
			}
		}
	}

			industrial_capacity_dockyard = 0.10
		}
